<div>
<article property="schema:text"><div id="home-page">
<div id="top-photo">
<div id="red-left">
<h2>Millions of students don't get an accurate science education</h2>
<a href="/research">How do we know?</a></div>
<div id="blue-right">
<h2>The National Center for Science Education believes they deserve better.</h2>
</div>
</div>
<div id="problem-chart">
<div id="chart-left">
<h3 class="eyebrow">The Root of the Problem</h3>
<h2>Science teachers recognize that evolution and climate change are widely misunderstood or rejected in many places.</h2>
<p>Teaching these topics without specific training is challenging. Many teachers avoid these well-established yet culturally controversial areas of science to avoid conflict.</p>
</div>
<div id="cart-right">
<span class="side-60"><img alt="" data-entity-type="" data-entity-uuid="" src="/sites/all/themes/evol/images/60.png"/>
<p><strong>60%</strong> of high school biology teachers teach evolution inaccurately or inadequately.</p>
<a href="/evolution-and-creationism-americas-classrooms-national-portrait">Read the Study</a></span>
<span class="side-40"> <img alt="" data-entity-type="" data-entity-uuid="" src="/sites/all/themes/evol/images/40.png"/>
<p><strong>40%</strong> of middle and high school teachers teach climate change inaccurately.</p>
<a href="/mixed-messages-how-climate-change-taught-americas-public-schools">Read the Study</a> </span></div>
</div>
<div class="get-involved-page" id="middle-blue">
<div id="video-section">
<h3 class="eyebrow">How We Help</h3>
<div class="hold-video">
<div class="embed-container">
<iframe allow="accelerometer; autoplay; encrypted-media; gyroscope; picture-in-picture" allowfullscreen="" frameborder="0" src="https://www.youtube.com/embed/IyLfcNaeB7Y"></iframe></div>
</div>
<h2>The National Center for Science Education:</h2>
</div>
<div class="support top-row"><a href="/supporting-teachers">Supports Teachers</a></div>
<div class="science top-row"><a href="/breaking-down-barriers">Engages Communities</a></div>
<div class="ask top-row"><a href="/catalyzing-action">Catalyzes Action</a></div>
</div>
<div class="body-hero"><img alt="" src="/sites/all/themes/evol/images/home-hero.jpg"/></div>
</div>
<div class="full-width blue" id="home-map">
<div class="wrapper">
<h2 class="eyebrow">Our Impact</h2>
<h3 class="purple">Supporting Teachers</h3>
<div class="home-map-block purple">
<div class="home-map-number">47</div>
<div class="home-map-text">
				Teacher Ambassadors across 29 states and Puerto Rico
			</div>
</div>
<h3 class="yellow">Breaking Down Barriers</h3>
<div class="home-map-block yellow">
<div class="home-map-number">10</div>
<div class="home-map-text">
				Graduate Student Outreach Fellows in 7 states
			</div>
</div>
<h3 class="green">Catalyzing Action</h3>
